password/Confirm password、按钮 \"Update password\"；空 current 显示 \"Current password is required\"；错误文案按 REQ-1-3；成功显示 \"Password updated\"，旧密码立即失效。\n6. password/Confirm password、按钮 \"Update password\"；空 current 显示 \"Current password is required\"；错误文案按 REQ-1-3；成功显示 \"Password updated\"，旧密码立即失效。\n6. 路径；`sign-in`/`sign-out` 已由 PR #2 发布并被前端 client、单测与 e2e 使用，故保持 canonical、不加别名。\n7. 种子：docs/seed-data.md 账户表写入 seed 注册表（PR #2 已满足，本任务仅核实）。\n\n## 前置\n基础 PR #2 已合入 develop（脚手架、会话骨架、copy.ts、seed The tool Node on\n> the default PATH is a different major version, and `better-sqlite3` has no prebuilt binary for it,\n> so a plain `pnpm install` silently compiles a native module for the wrong Node and it will not load\n> under the delivered runtime.\n\nThe delivered application is installed per directory by the platform:\n\n```bash\ncd frontend && npm install && npm run build\ncd backend  && npm install && HOST=0.0.0.0 PORT=3000 npm run start\n```\n\n`backend` is plain ESM JavaScript on purpose: `npm run start` works **without** a build step, and\n`frontend/dist` is served by the same process with an SPA fallback.\n\n## Development\n\n```bash\npnpm dev            # both services through portless\npnpm dev:api        # backend only  (http://<worktree>.pr2-api.localhost)\npnpm dev:web        # frontend only (http://<worktree>.pr2-web.localhost, proxies /api)\n```\n\nportless injects `HOST`/`PORT`; the backend additionally accepts `DB_PATH` (default\n`backend/data/app.db`) and `SEED=0` to skip seeding.\n\nThe dev servers are launched with the delivery Node (`APP_NODE`, default `/usr/local/bin/node`) because the\ntoolchain in this environment runs under a different Node major version — `better-sqlite3` is built for one\nABI only. `pnpm dev` prints the binary it uses."

The system validates input rules, displays field errors, and prevents duplicate accounts; on success, it directly creates an available account. Submitting several invalid fields together must show the username, email, password, and missing-terms messages together, rather than revealing only one error per submission; the submit button remains actionable so these messages can be read. For example, a username beginning with a hyphen, email “not-an-email”, password “short”, confirmation “different”, and unchecked terms produce these field errors and preserve the attempted username. A duplicate username paired with a different unused email displays “Username already exists” and retains both attempted values. A successful registration accepts a unique username such as “pw-user-<unique suffix>”, its “@example.test” email, and password “Valid-password-123!”; the sign-in form is immediately available, accepts that email, and the resulting signed-in username remains visible after reload.\n\nScreenshot reference:\n![image](reference/github-sign-up.png)\n================================================================================\nREQ-1-1-2 | Sign In with an Existing Account\nThis feature builds on the persisted objects, opened pages, or authenticated-session context provided by REQ-1-1-1 (Register a New GitHub Account); it must reuse that context and keep pages and data consistent after the context changes. The sign-in page is the form on the account-access page containing “Username or email”, “Password”, and “Sign in”. A “session” is the current-browser login record saved after successful account authentication, represented in the frontend by the upper-right account menu and access to protected pages. The visitor enters a username or email and a password and submits the form. The system looks up the account and validates its credentials and availability status; if authentication fails due to an unknown username/email, incorrect password, or unavailable account, the system displays exactly the same generic failure message “Invalid credentials”, does not disclose whether the account exists or which credential was wrong, and does not create a session. After successful authentication, the system stores a session with a unique session identifier, account identifier, and active status, then redirects to the workspace accessible to that user; the session remains valid after refreshing or reopening the workspace, and subsequent protected organization, repository, and work-item requests must use this session to determine the current user.\n\nScreenshot reference:\n![image](reference/github-sign-in.png)\n================================================================================\nREQ-1-1-3 | Recover Account Access Through a Verified Email\nThe password-recovery page is opened by the link “Forgot password” on the sign-in page; the fixed code is displayed as a distinct visible text value exactly “123456”, not only embedded inside a longer instruction. “Email”, “Verification code”, “New password”, and “Confirm password” are associated field labels; “Send reset link” and “Reset password” are buttons. The recovery scenario uses a newly registered account, an unknown email, a compliant replacement such as “Replacement-password-456!”, and invalid code “000000”; failure leaves the registered account able to sign in with its old email/password. In this flow, the local system does not send email, generate a copyable reset link, or call an external verification-code service. After the visitor enters an address in the Email field and clicks “Send reset link”, the page directly switches to the next step of the same recovery flow, displays the fixed verification-code text “123456” for local demonstration only, and shows visibly labeled Verification code, New password, Confirm password fields and a “Reset password” button; the page must not require access to email. To prevent disclosure through the page of whether an account exists, both registered and unknown email addresses enter the same next step and display the same fixed verification code. The system atomically updates the credentials of that account only when the recovery context is associated with a registered email, the Verification code is character-for-character equal to “123456”, the new password complies with the password rules in REQ-1-1-1, and Confirm password matches it. If the verification code is wrong, the email is unknown, the password is noncompliant, or the confirmation does not match, the page explains the reason beside the corresponding field and does not modify any account; an incorrect verification code displays “Verification code is invalid”. After successful submission, the page directly displays “Password updated” and does not produce any email or link. This local verification code has no email-delivery, expiration, or one-time-link semantics. Password fields must not echo entered values.\n\nScreenshot reference:\n![image](reference/github-password-reset.png) ![image](reference/github-password-reset-1.png)\n================================================================================\nREQ-1-2 | Sign Out and End the Current Web Session\nThis feature builds on the persisted objects, opened pages, or authenticated-session context provided by REQ-1-1-2 (Sign In with an Existing Account); it must reuse that context and keep pages and data consistent after the context changes. The page contains exactly one button named “Account menu”; its menu contains exactly one link named “Sign out”. Activating it displays a dialog named “Sign out” with buttons named “Confirm sign out” and “Cancel”. The dialog explains that sign-out affects only the current browser session. Only “Confirm sign out” invalidates the session, while “Cancel” or closing the dialog retains the current session and page. After confirming, refresh, browser back navigation, or directly reopening a previously accessible protected account, repository, or organization page restores an unauthenticated state and displays the “Sign in” link.\n\nScreenshot reference:\n![image](reference/github-account-menu-sign-out.png)\n================================================================================\nREQ-1-3 | Change Account Password\nThis feature builds on the persisted objects, opened pages, or authenticated-session context provided by REQ-1-1-2 (Sign In with an Existing Account); it must reuse that context and keep pages and data consistent after the context changes. A failure with an incorrect current password and confirmation “does-not-match” must display the corresponding current-password or confirmation error and leave the old credentials usable. Each password-change scenario starts with its own verified account in the stated credential state and a different compliant candidate password. “Password and authentication” is the security page in account Settings used to change the current account credentials; the change affects only the current account's password record and does not alter any other accounts or resources outside the session. The user must provide the current password, a new password compliant with the REQ-1 password rules, and a character-for-character identical confirmation password; all three fields are required. The system validates the change, prevents unauthorized updates, and applies the new password to subsequent sign-in attempts. If the current password is incorrect, the new password is noncompliant, the confirmation does not match, or any field is missing, the system displays the reason in the corresponding field and does not modify the credentials; the applicable visible message is “Current password is incorrect”, “Password confirmation does not match”, or “Current password is required”. Playwright：5 条需求对应用例（含刷新持久化、密码不回显、已知/未知邮箱一致）。\n\n## 设计（供 PR 负责人实施／复核）\n\n### API（全部 `POST /api/auth/*`，错误体遵循 §3：`errors[]` 一次返回全部字段错误）\n\n| 端点 | 认证 | 请求体 | 成功 | 字段错误（`field` → 文案） |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `register`（已有） | 无 | `username,email,password,confirmPassword,agreeToTerms` | 201 `{user}` | `username`→`Username is required`/`Username format is invalid`/`Username credentials`（含字段缺失） |\n| `sign-out`（已有） | cookie 可选 | — | 200 `{ok}` + 清 cookie | — |\n| `forgot-password` | 无 | `email` | 200 `{ok:true}`（对已注册与未注册邮箱响应完全相同） | `email`→`Email is required`/`Email format is invalid` |\n| `reset-password` | 无 | `email,code,newPassword,confirmPassword` | 200 `{ok:true, message:'Password updated'}` | `code`→`Verification code is invalid`；`newPassword`→`Password is required`/`Password requirements are not satisfied`；`confirmPassword`→`Confirm password is required`/`Password confirmation does not match`；未注册邮箱→按 `code` 字段的非泄露处理（见决定 D2） |\n| `change-password` | `requireAuth` | `currentPassword,newPassword,confirmPassword` | 200 `{ok:true, message:'Password updated'}` | `currentPassword`→`Current password is required`（空）/`Current password is incorrect`（错误）；`newPassword`→`Password is required`/`Password requirements are not satisfied`；`confirmPassword`→`Confirm password is required`/`Password confirmation does not match`；未登录→401 `Authentication required` |\n\n- 未在需求中出现的字段错误文案不得自造（例如不为“未注册邮箱”引入新文案）。\n- 任何响应不得包含 `password_hash`；密码字段不回显（前端提交失败后清空、且不回填）。\n- `reset-password` / `change-password` 成功后在**同一事务**内更新 `password_hash` 并调用\n  `revokeSessionsForUser()`（语义见 D1 / D1b）。`reset-password` 无服务端待处理状态（不需要 recovery 表）：\n  “恢复上下文”= 提交时的 `email` + `code`，服务端重新校验。`forgot-password` 只做邮箱格式校验，\n  不查询、不泄露账户是否存在。\n\n### 前端\n\n- `/password-reset`（由登录页链接 `Forgot password` 打开；步骤由 URL 驱动，见 D3）\n  - 第 1 步：`Email` 字段 + `Send reset link` 按钮（+ 返回 `Sign in` 链接）。\n  - 第 2 步（`?step=2&email=…`）：固定验证码文本 `123456` 作为**独立可见元素**（绝不嵌入其它句子，\n    保证按精确文本可断言），`Verification code` / `New password` / `Confirm password` 字段 + `Reset password` 按钮。\n  - 失败：按 `errors[]` 在对应字段旁逐字显示；成功后（`?step=done`）显示 `Password updated`（不跳转、不产生邮件/链接）。\n  - 密码字段提交失败后清空、不回显。\n- `/settings/*`：改为嵌套路由。`Password and authentication` 链接进入 `/settings/password`，渲染安全表单\n  （`Current password` / `New password` / `Confirm password` + `Update password`），成功后显示 `Password updated`，\n  失败按字段显示错误；空 current 显示 `Current password is required`。\n- 其余页面（注册/登录/账户菜单/登出确认）保持 PR #2 行为，仅按验收补断言。\n\n## 决定（Issue #3）\n\n经过原生 advisor 咨询（2026-09-29，见“依据”），确定如下。**关键事实：需求原文从不规定 API 路径**，全部断言都是页面级的\n（accessible name、可见文案、登录/登出行为），因此 API 形状按已发布契约与最小改动决定。\n\n- **D1 改密后的会话语义：保留调用者当前会话，只吊销该用户的其它会话**\n  （`revokeSessionsForUser(db, user.id, { exceptSessionId: req.sessionId })`）。理由：REQ-1-3 场景 1 的流程是\n  “改密成功 → 页面显示 `Password updated` → 从账户菜单 `Sign out` → 用新密码登录”，把操作者登出会使该流程与断言失效；\n  架构 §3 的“改密即时失效会话”按“其它会话不得继续有效”理解。\n- **D1b 找回密码成功：吊销该用户全部会话**（`revokeSessionsForUser` 不带 except，恢复流程是未认证的，没有当前会话要保），\n  **不自动登录、不建新会话**；成功后只显示 `Password updated`，后续用新密码走正常登录。\n- **D2 未知邮箱在恢复第 2 步：与错误验证码完全相同的表现** —— `Verification code is invalid`（字段 `code`）。\n  理由：不泄露句是双重表述（description 专句 + 场景 1 THEN「do not indicate whether the email exists」），而需求只为\n  “错误验证码”规定了确切文案，未给未知邮箱任何文案，隐藏验收无法断言不存在的字符串。无论哪条路径都**不得修改任何账户**。\n- **D3 恢复流程步骤由 URL 驱动**：`/password-reset`（第 1 步）→ `/password-reset?step=2&email=…`（第 2 步，显示独立文本\n  `123456` 与重置表单）→ 成功后 `/password-reset?step=done`（显示 `Password updated`）。\n  - `123456` 来自 `copy.ts` 的 `VERIFICATION_CODE`，**不在 mount 时发任何请求**：刷新/重新打开只重渲染，\n    天然满足“刷新不产生邮件/链接/第二个账户”，并覆盖“刷新后结果仍可见”的通用 THEN。\n  - 第 1 步 `Send reset link` 调 `POST /api/auth/forgot-password` 仅做邮箱格式校验（已注册/未注册邮箱响应完全相同），\n    成功后才跳转；`?step=2` 缺少 `email` 时回落第 1 步。直接访问 `?step=2` 允许（验证码是本地固定值，不泄露存在性）。\n- **D4 接口命名：保留已发布的 `sign-in`/`sign-out` 为 canonical，不添加 `login`/`logout` 别名**；\n  Issue #3 交付清单里的 `login|logout` 按笔误更正（需求原文未规定路径，基础契约、前端 client、单测与 e2e 均已使用 sign-in/sign-out；\n  加别名只会引入无消费者的第二契约面）。新端点用既有风格：`/api/auth/forgot-password`、`/api/auth/reset-password`、\n  `/api/auth/change-password`。\n- **D5 文案**：只使用 `copy.ts` 已定义的常量；需求未给出的错误文案不自造。找回成功/改密成功都显示 `Password updated`。\n- **D6 seed 账户保护**：Playwright 用例**不得修改 seed 账户密码**（seed 为 `ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING`，\n  改动会在同一次 e2e 运行的共享临时库中残留并影响其它用例）。改密/找回用例一律先注册唯一 `pw-*` 新账户。\n- **D7 多项错误**：新端点在一次提交内返回全部字段错误（与 §3 一致），前端逐字段渲染；\n  例如改密“current 错 + 确认不匹配”两条同时显示。\n- **D8 成功后原地显示，不自动跳转**：`Password updated` 必须在当前页显示（需求用的是 “directly displays”），\n  自动 navigate 会造成断言竞态。\n- **D9 密码不回显**：注册/找回/改密提交失败后清空所有密码字段（保留 username/email/identifier 等非敏感输入），\n  成功后也清空；任何响应不含 `password_hash`。\n- **D10 `123456` 是独立可见文本节点**（需求：“not only embedded inside a longer instruction”）：\n  单独元素渲染，任何说明句中都不得再出现这串数字。\n- **D11 不做需求外的校验**：例如“新密码必须不同于旧密码”——需求未规定，不得添加。\n- **D12 邮箱归一化一致**：注册/登录/找回/重置一律复用 `validateEmail`（trim + 小写）；\n  密码不做 trim/大小写处理、逐字符比较。\n- **D13 不给密码字段加可见性切换等额外控件**（需求写 “exactly one” 的字段/按钮，额外同名控件会破坏唯一性断言）。\n- **D14 登出后的不可恢复链**：`signOut()` 清空客户端用户状态；`RequireAuth` 以服务端 `GET /api/auth/session` 为准，\n  保证刷新/后退/直达受保护页都回到未认证态并显示 `Sign in`。\n\n## 验收方案（模块级，docs/acceptance-plan.md 分层）\n\nPlaywright（`e2e/identity.spec.ts`，新）：每条需求一组用例，`getByRole(role,{name})` 精确名称、文案逐字断言、\n每条用例新 context 起始（fresh unauthenticated）、对象名带唯一后缀、断言用唯一 token 限定范围。\n\n高风险点（advisor 咨询后列出的自有断言，务必覆盖）：引号名逐条唯一存在（strict mode）——`Create an account` 链接、\n`Account menu` 按钮、`Sign out` 链接（必须是 link role，不能是 menuitem）、各字段 textbox；\n`123456` 按精确文本命中；成功后不跳转；失败后密码输入为空、username/email 保留；多项错误同时显示；\n登出后刷新/后退/直达受保护页都要重新认证；每类失败都补“旧密码仍可登录 / 候选新密码不可登录”的端到端断言；\n恢复第 2 步刷新时不得发出写请求（用 `page.on('request')` 断言无 `POST /api/auth/*`）且 `123456` 仍可见。\n\n- REQ-1-1-1：注册成功 → 进入登录页、邮箱可登录、账户菜单显示用户名、刷新仍在；\n  “-bad / not-an-email / short / different / 未勾选”一次提交显示 username/email/password/confirm/terms 五条文案并保留\n  username+email、密码输入为空、URL 仍在 `/register`；重复用户名显示 `Username already exists` 且保留两个输入；\n  失败提交后该凭据无法登录（`Invalid credentials`）→ 未创建账户。\n- REQ-1-1-2：用 username 与 email 分别登录成功；未知账户/错误密码都只显示 `Invalid credentials` 且无账户菜单；\n  登录后刷新受保护页仍显示用户名；未登录直达受保护页要求认证。\n- REQ-1-1-3：新注册账户走恢复流程（已知邮箱）：第 2 步显示独立文本 `123456`；错误码 `000000` →\n  `Verification code is invalid`、旧密码仍可登录、候选新密码不可登录；正确码 + 合规新密码 → `Password updated`，\n  之后旧密码登录失败、新密码成功。未知邮箱：同样进入第 2 步、同样显示 `123456`、不泄露存在性；\n  刷新恢复页不产生邮件/链接/新账户。密码不回显。\n- REQ-1-2：确认登出后未认证（刷新、浏览器后退、直达受保护页都要求重新认证并显示 `Sign in`）；\n  `Cancel` 或关闭对话框保留会话与原页面。\n- REQ-1-3：成功流（改密 → `Password updated` → 登出 → 新密码可登录、旧密码失败）；空 current →\n  `Current password is required`；错误 current → `Current password is incorrect`；不合规新密码 →\n  `Password requirements are not satisfied`；确认不一致 → `Password confirmation does not match`；\n  失败后旧密码仍可登录；新密码不回显。\n\n后端 Vitest（`backend/test/`，扩展 `auth.test.js` 或新增 `identity-recovery.test.js`）：\n每个新端点的成功/字段错误/多项错误一次返回、非泄露（已注册与未注册邮箱响应逐字节一致）、\n重置后旧会话失效（同用户两个会话都失效）、改密后旧密码不能登录而新密码可以、响应不含 `password_hash`。\n\n整合验收（不在本任务）：develop → main 整合 PR 由根负责人执行全量 `pnpm e2e` 与平台启动路径冒烟。\n\n## 依据（原生 advisor 咨询，2026-09-29）\n\n- 需求原文不规定任何 API 路径 → 命名以已发布契约为准（D4）。\n- D1 的反例分析：登出操作者会使 REQ-1-3 场景 1 的“改密成功后从账户菜单登出”流程与 `Account menu` 断言直接失败；\n  且 `revokeSessionsForUser()` 自带的 `exceptSessionId` 参数本身就是为基础 PR 预留的“保留当前会话”用法。\n- D2 的强度比较：不泄露句有 description 专句 + 场景 THEN 双重表述；需求只为错误验证码给出确切文案。\n- D3 的取舍：URL 状态同时覆盖“刷新不产生副作用”与“刷新后 `123456`/成功文案仍可见”两种可能断言；\n  代价是直接访问 `?step=2` 也能看到表单（需求未禁止，且验证码是本地演示值）。\n\n## 未决问题 / 风险\n\n- **待根 Issue #1 确认（不阻塞实现）**：D4 对交付清单端点名的更正；以及是否把 D1/D1b 的会话语义、D2 的非泄露处理\n  作为澄清写进 `docs/architecture.md` §3（或 §9 追加条目）。若确认，由 PR #3 一并更新文档；未确认前以本 packet caught : new ApiError(0, { code: 'unknown', message: errorCopy.unknown }));\n    } finally {\n      setSubmitting(false);\n    }\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"mx-auto w-full max-w-sm mt-10\">\n      <h1 className=\"page-title text-center\">{authCopy.forgotPassword}</h1>\n\n      <div className=\"app-card p-4 mt-4\">\n        {error ? 咨询（2026-09-29，见“依据”），确定如下。**关键事实：需求原文从不规定 API 路径**，全部断言都是页面级的\n（accessible name、可见文案、登录/登出行为），因此 API 形状按已发布契约与最小改动决定。\n\n- **D1 改密后的会话语义：保留调用者当前会话，只吊销该用户的其它会话**\n  （`revokeSessionsForUser(db, user.id, { exceptSessionId: req.sessionId })`）。理由：REQ-1-3 场景 1 的流程是\n  “改密成功 → 页面显示 `Password updated` → 从账户菜单 `Sign out` → 用新密码登录”，把操作者登出会使该流程与断言失效；\n  架构 §3 的“改密即时失效会话”按“其它会话不得继续有效”理解。\n- **D1b 找回密码成功：吊销该用户全部会话**（`revokeSessionsForUser` 不带 except，恢复流程是未认证的，没有当前会话要保），\n  **不自动登录、不建新会话**；成功后只显示 `Password updated`，后续用新密码走正常登录。\n- **D2 未知邮箱在恢复第 2 步：与错误验证码完全相同的表现** —— `Verification code is invalid`（字段 `code`）。\n  理由：不泄露句是双重表述（description 专句 + 场景 1 THEN「do not indicate whether the email exists」），而需求只为\n  “错误验证码”规定了确切文案，未给未知邮箱任何文案，隐藏验收无法断言不存在的字符串。无论哪条路径都**不得修改任何账户**。\n- **D3 恢复流程步骤由 URL 驱动**：`/password-reset`（第 1 步）→ `/password-reset?step=2&email=…`（第 2 步，显示独立文本\n  `123456` 与重置表单）→ 成功后 `/password-reset?step=done`（显示 `Password updated`）。\n  - `123456` 来自 `copy.ts` 的 成功后才跳转；`?step=2` 缺少 `email` 时回落第 1 步。直接访问 `?step=2` 允许（验证码是本地固定值，不泄露存在性）。\n- **D4 接口命名：保留已发布的 `sign-in`/`sign-out` 为 canonical，不添加 `login`/`logout` 别名**；\n  Issue #3 交付清单里的 `login|logout` 按笔误更正（需求原文未规定路径，基础契约、前端 client、单测与 e2e 均已使用 sign-in/sign-out；\n  加别名只会引入无消费者的第二契约面）。新端点用既有风格：`/api/auth/forgot-password`、`/api/auth/reset-password`、\n  `/api/auth/change-password`。\n- **D5 文案**：只使用 `copy.ts` 已定义的常量；需求未给出的错误文案不自造。找回成功/改密成功都显示 `Password updated`。\n- **D6 seed 账户保护**：Playwright 用例**不得修改 seed 账户密码**（seed 为 `ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING`，\n  改动会在同一次 e2e 运行的共享临时库中残留并影响其它用例）。改密/找回用例一律先注册唯一 `pw-*` 新账户。\n- **D7 多项错误**：新端点在一次提交内返回全部字段错误（与 §3 一致），前端逐字段渲染；\n  例如改密“current 错 + 确认不匹配”两条同时显示。\n- **D8 成功后原地显示，不自动跳转**：`Password updated` 必须在当前页显示（需求用的是 “directly displays”），\n  自动 navigate 会造成断言竞态。\n- **D9 密码不回显**：注册/找回/改密提交失败后清空所有密码字段（保留 username/email/identifier 等非敏感输入），\n  成功后也清空；任何响应不含 `password_hash`。\n- **D10 `123456` 是独立可见文本节点**（需求：“not only embedded inside a longer instruction”）：\n  单独元素渲染，任何说明句中都不得再出现这串数字。\n- **D11 不做需求外的校验**：例如“新密码必须不同于旧密码”——需求未规定，不得添加。\n- **D12 邮箱归一化一致**：注册/登录/找回/重置一律复用 `validateEmail`（trim + 小写）；\n  密码不做 trim/大小写处理、逐字符比较。\n- **D13 不给密码字段加可见性切换等额外控件**（需求写 “exactly one” 的字段/按钮，额外同名控件会破坏唯一性断言）。\n- **D14 登出后的不可恢复链**：`signOut()` 清空客户端用户状态；`RequireAuth` 以服务端 `GET /api/auth/session` 为准，\n  保证刷新/后退/直达受保护页都回到未认证态并显示 `Sign in`。\n\n##
